Mr. President, the lawmakers naturally set the pandemic for the first order of the public health agenda in these difficult times when humanity is struggling with Covid-19. This unexpected outbreak shows once again the inequality of the global health system while teaching humanity that their health systems are not ready for an epidemic of this magnitude. Because of this epidemic, more than 3.5 million people have died in the world. The number of our citizens who lost their lives in our country exceeded 45,000. Every evening, we watch statistics reflected in the Department of Health's turquoise table with sadness and anxiety. The spreader of the Covid-19 outbreak is the new type of coronavirus. How many people die every day because of the tobacco epidemic spreading through multinational cigarette companies? What costs does the tobacco pandemic, which deepens inequality in health systems, makes socioeconomic disadvantages even more vulnerable to humanity and our country? Let's try to understand with this painting: the number of people who smoke in the world is 1.3 billion cases and 20 million in Turkey. Because of smoking, 8 million people die every year in the world, 22,000 people die every day. We lose about 120,000 citizens in our country every year because of cigarettes, so 328 citizens die every day because of cigarettes. Congressmans, the effects of the cigarette epidemic are not only harmful to humanity, but the environmental and economic costs it has paid for humanity. In our country alone, a total annual economic loss from cigarettes reaches $50 billion. This data reminds us once again of the importance of fighting the tobacco pandemic without desensitizing. Congressmans, in order to continue an effective fight against the tobacco epidemic, we need to understand the role of multinational cigarette companies in spreading the epidemic. Even though they've been responsible for the deaths of millions of people over the years, how can cigarette giants still make a lot of money? To answer that question, you need to know the tactics of the tobacco industry. The propaganda activities of cigarette companies are concentrated in three areas -- increasing cigarette sales, preventing legal regulations, a masterful avoidance of their criticism and responsibility. To this end, they have developed many tactics, such as affecting consumer behavior with media manipulations, developing products and campaigns for vulnerable target masses, including children, trying to gain trust through social responsibility projects, blocking active policies with lobbying activities. By pretending to be part of the solution, companies use their new toxins in the field with a lie about reduced products. In particular, heated tobacco products and electronic cigarettes that have recently been cited are new tools for these manipulations. Fortunately, tobacco products and e-cigaries, electronic cigarettes, which have been heated by our President's initiative, have been prevented from entering and selling our country. (ARK PARTY) With this determination, a new outbreak of youth, as in the example of America, has been prevented from spreading into our country, reaching an alarmous dimension. The legal arrangement and strategy proposals the World Health Organization has proposed against the industry's tactics are clear. In the beginning, it's to make all closed public areas 100% smoke-free. Let's not forget that nearly a million people die every year because he's been exposed to cigarette smoke, even though he doesn't smoke. Another effective policy proposal is to impose high taxes on tobacco products. Increased cigarette taxes is one of the most efficient tools in the community, especially to protect children and young people. Scientific studies show that even a 10% increase in cigarette prices reduces the use of cigarettes in children and young people by 15 percent. Obstruction of all advertising and marketing activities of cigarette companies is another effective policy that is recommended to be widespread and accessible to the public, to be constantly informed about the harm of smoking.

In particular, there was a question about what was involved in the proposal of the law, so if you'll excuse me, I'll answer it. The public administrations considered under this law offer include the Treasury and Finance Ministry tax offices, the Ministry of Commerce customs offices, the Social Security Institution, the urban municipalities, municipalities, municipal private administrations, YİKOPs and some occupational institutions. If we look at what's available in this range, other than taxes, pictures, tuition and tax fines and tariffs on them and interest, administrative fines, except for tobacco, the Hafzzishaha Act and the higher board fines; student loans followed by Law Penalty 6183; loans such as SGO bonuses, tariffs, tariffs, increases and interest; municipalities' taxes, fees and water payments; the state's private administrations will receive; Investment and Coordinations, such as NGOs, some professions, such as NGOS, TMBS, TBS, TBS, TBS, TBS. I'd like to open this up. The issues related to occupational institutions are entirely their own demands, no sanctions have been made, and those who make written requests from occupational organisations, with respect for their internal affairs and decisions, are structured within this scope. In the configuration of fixed payments, all of the loans will be delayed interest, the amount upgraded to domestic HFE, instead of an increase, and the tax-related fines will be paid by 50 per cent of which are not subject to tax-related penalties, and the collection of the tax fines will be waived. The proportions based on the domestic HFE upgrade with the law have been set as 0.35 per cent per month and 4.2 per cent per year since 1/11/2016. It has also been covered by the undeclared or court-ordered clients. The release of the law has been determined by date that the period of trial has not expired, or that there will be lawsuits, and the amount of measures to be paid at different rates will be taken into consideration. For example, if the first level of trial has not expired or the trial has not yet been settled, 50 per cent of the tax base will be paid, instead of the delay interest, and instead of the increase, it will be updated with the domestic UFE. The remaining tax is to be waived from collecting tax fines, tariffs, and interest. As for those in the study process, they can also provide configuration for the taxpayers who continue to study the law as of the date it was published. The taxpayers will be able to benefit from the law after the completion of the proceedings. In this case, if they apply for 50 per cent of the tax base on the notice to be issued to them, the tax penalty, delay hikes, and, instead of tariffs, they will not be able to pay the amount that has been updated to the domestic UFE, the tax will be waived from the collection of tax fines, tariffs, and interest rates. There was another question about the Matrah. In the matter of the Matrah and tax increase, the taxpayers' income tax returns from 2016 to 2020 are organised to ensure that there will be no tax reviews and tax tariffs on their rights, no income and institutions' tax returns, and no more income and investment taxes, provided they increase their value taxes in the law at an estimated rate or minimum, and at a certain rate of tax payments. In this scope, the income and institutional tax taxpayers will be able to benefit from the annual declarations they gave, until 31/8/2021 -- including this date -- 35 per cent for the 2016 calendar year, 2017 for the calendar year, 25 per cent for the 2018 calendar year, 20 per cent for the calendar year 20, 2020 calendar year, but not less than 15 per cent for the year of the calendar year. The increases will be taxed by 20 per cent and no taxes will be taken on it. In addition, the law proposal includes administrative fines that have not been finalized with the lack of labour premiums, which are in the process of preliminary evaluation, research or detection of social security agencies. A comprehensive arrangement has been made. Why is this arrangement so comprehensive? Because the whole world and Turkey are entering a new era. From the conditions of the pandemic we have lived in for a long time, we are entering what we call a period of postpandemia. It's a transitional process, of course, that doesn't just happen. Inoculation studies in particular indicate that a new era will begin throughout the world and in our country. We're going through a period of recovery and normalisation in economies. So, in the search of starting this new era as taxpayers and as a public-private relationship, in a sense, in a much more appropriate way, this bill has come before our Parliament and has been discussed in our General Assembly. It's not a tax pardon, it's always protected. On the other hand, there's a concept we call a dutiful taxpayer. We expect that if our council passes by by the will of its captors, it will have benefits for both our taxpayers and for the public. Thank you.

tbmm-2021-06-02sit03spe0174par0105	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on behalf of the Nationalist Movement Party Group on the first part of the Law Proposal for Reconstruction of Some Laws and some Laws. I salute the Veteran Assembly and our humble citizens who follow us. The offer we're discussing is a very comprehensive arrangement for public pay and is an important provision for the relief of our citizens who are unable to meet their debt obligations in time due to the epidemic. As you may recall, the Law No. 7256, published on 17 November 2020, also provided the opportunity to pay off certain debts in configuration and installments. However, a new arrangement is being made to ensure that the effects of the epidemic continue, that our citizens, who are unable to pay their installments or whose debts are not covered by configuration, will also be given an opportunity to ease the problems that have arisen due to the stagnation of commercial life. The law proposal envisions the restructuring of the agreed-upon futures and debts in the phase of the trial and in the process of review, the marah and tax increase, and the correction of business records. Tax offices, customs administrations, Social Security Institution, major city municipalities, municipalities, municipal private administrations, the President of Investment Watch and Co-ordination and some vocational organisations are being structured in this regard to pre-date 30 April 2021. Taxes, pictures, tuition and such are covered by tax fines, administrative fines, student loans, tuitions, SGK bonuses, tariff hikes and interest, municipalities' tax, wages and water, private administrations will receive, tradesmen and artists and other members of the room and stock markets, pension loans from lawyers and paralegal lawyers. The criminal fines and the tobacco law on Hüfzizishahahah and the fine imposed on the upper boards are excluded from the scope of configuration. In the restructuring of other presumptions within the scope of the SGK, instead of the delay interest of all debt bases, you will keep updating with Homeland ÜFE, and the payment of 50% of non-tax-related fines will be waived on the condition that tax sentences are paid by delays and the collection of interest. The upgrade process includes a lower rate of monthly change in the Internal Price Index until November 1, 2016, and a much lower interest rate, based on 4.2 per cent per year after this date. The final application date for use of configuration has been set on August 31st 2021, and the payment is scheduled to be made in two months in advance or in the form of 6, 9, 12, 18. The payments will be made to the tax offices, customs offices, municipal private administrations, municipalities and Investment Watch and Co-ordination Presidencys by September 30, 2021, and to Social Security Agency by October 31, 2021. The coefficient will not be applied if the entire amount is paid in advance during the initial payment period, and instead of the benefits, 90 per cent of the amount will be calculated on the basis of domestic UNE monthly changes, and 50 per cent will be waived if the first two installments are paid during the payment period. In the event that the result of the configuration is only ferli payables, you will be given a 50% reduction in the amount which will be calculated on the basis of domestic UNE monthly change rates. In the fixed benefits of the Social Security Agency, the premiums for April 2021 and the previous months are waived from collecting all of those who will receive the benefits, such as a delay and a delay increase, based on the domestic manufacturer's price index monthly change rates. Another arrangement made with the proposal is intended for the increase in mathematics and taxes. Tax reviews and tax tariffs will not be issued for them, as long as the taxpayers will pay their income and institutional taxes from 2016 to 2020, by increasing them by the rate envisioned in the law, and by limiting their years and tax types. The increases will be taxed by 20 per cent and no taxes will be taken on it. For tax-compatible taxpayers, the rate will be 15 per cent. The offer also allows businesses to adjust stock and safe deposit boxes and update their balance sheets until December 31st, 2021. As of April 30, 2021, it is also part of the proposal to halt the insurance times of BUG-KUR and agricultural insurance holders who owe premiums and do not pay. Unrequited checks, protestive bonds, credit cards and other loans are also part of regulations concerning past payment performances, known in public, if the Turkish Union of Banks fulfils its obligations to the Risk Centre and its credit customers because they are unable to pay their dues on time. The envisioned arrangements are measures that will improve the dynamics of economic life, ease the burden of our citizens, and also increase public revenue collection capacity, in accordance with the expectations of the communities. Dear lawmakers, despite the restrictive effects of the epidemic, our economic activities remain strong. In the first quarter of 2021, Turkey's economy grew by 7 per cent. Developments also indicate strong growth in the second quarter. In the first quarter, the industrial sector grew 11.7 per cent, while the manufacturing industry grew at 12.2 per cent, while the machine and equipment investments increased by 30.5 per cent. From the second half of the year 2020, the trend in exports continues to be strong. In April, our exports increased by 109.2 per cent from the same month of the previous year to $18.7 billion, and increased by 65.5 billion in May, according to the Trade Ministry's statement today. The measures to protect employment limit the epidemic's negative effects on the labour market, while employment and participation in the labour force are also increasing. At the same time, the continuation of the epidemic limits the recovery of the service sector, particularly the tourism sector. For this reason, a number of important support measures have been put into place for the tradesman, the farmer, and our employees. Müfür developments point to a healthy investment, production, export and employment chain, showing confidence in the economy. While many countries' economies are shrinking, the high performance of the Turkish economy is certainly satisfactory for our country. It should not be forgotten that Turkey has achieved this recovery simultaneously, as well as its decisive fight against terrorism. In our view, the consideration of this issue will be a righteous assessment that reveals the importance and meaning of the success achieved. Turkey, health and social security infrastructure, strong institutions, major projects around the world, the benefits of the presidential government system, which has been passed on by the will of the nation, will hopefully grow stronger and more determined to become the leading country and global power. With these thoughts, I wish the law's offer to be good for our country and our nation, and I salute the High Council.

Since the release of this Law has been filed prior to the date of the trial and/or court-ordered, and the investigation has been launched, including those who have been convicted, the case and/or the execution follow-up will be terminated if the borrower submits an application to take advantage of these joke judgments. In this case, the costs of the hearing and the costs of the execution and the power of attorney are not mutually required. The Union of Turkish Veterinary Doctors is in charge of the procedure and the basis for the implementation of this joke. Mustafa Elitaş Erkan Akçay Yunus Kılıç Sheikhmus Dinçel Mehmet Doğan Kubat
